def _fft_conv(a):
    n=len(a); out_len=2*n-1; N=1 << (out_len-1).bit_length(); A=scipy.fft.rfft(a,N); return scipy.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:out_len]
```

```
def _local_optimize(p, deadline, heat=0.1, step=0.5, beta=0.6, K=32, eval_every=50):
    n=len(p)
    z=np.log(p+1e-300)
    momentum=np.zeros(n)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score_fast(p)
    heat_current=heat
    for step_idx:
       p=softmax(z)
       b=conv(p,p)
       M=b.max()
       current_score=2nM
       if current_score<best_score: update
       # select top K indices
       # if K > len(b) K=len(b)
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       top_b=b[idx]
       # weights: if heat small, emphasize. Use stable over top:
       tau = max(1e-12, heat_current * M)
       # But top_b-M ≤0. logw=(top_b-M)/tau; w=exp(logw); normalize. If heat very small, only max gets weight; okay.

       for w, m in zip(weights, idx):

Pseudo local:
```
best_score = score_fast(p)
best_p = p.copy()
z = log(p)
momentum=0
heat=0.5
step=0.1
K=64
stalled=0
while time < deadline:
   p=softmax(z); b=conv; M; sc=2nM
   if sc < best_score-1e-12: best=...
   else stalled+=1
   # adaptive heat: if stalled, reset heat=0.3, z=perturb
   # select top K idx
   top = np_partition
   if K>1:
       tau = heat*M
       logw=(topb - M)/tau
       w=exp(logw); w/=w.sum()
   else w=1
   s=zeros; loop
   delta=s - dot(p,s)
   scale=max(abs(delta)); if scale==0 break
   d=delta/scale
   momentum = beta*momentum + (1-beta)*d
   z -= step*momentum; z-=z[0]
   heat *= heat_decay (towards min 0.01)
   step *= step_decay? maybe not.
```
But if objective smooth, step should perhaps decrease to converge. Use step_init 0.1, decay 0.999. If stuck reset.

Could use backtracking: after update, compute score; if worse than best by factor? Maybe keep z previous. Since we track best, if many worse, can revert to best z.    start from best_p with noise amplitude increasing, heat maybe.
   run until deadline portion.

def _local_optimize(p, name, deadline, heat_start=0.5, heat_end=0.02, K=64, step=0.1, beta=0.7, restart_s=60):
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_fast(p)
    current_start = max(0, deadline - time?)? We'll loop with restarts.
    while time < deadline:
        p_start = best_p * (1 + noise) normalized? or best_p + rng*std
        z = log(p_start)
        momentum=0
        heat=heat_start
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+restart_s)
        stalled=0
        while time < sub_deadline:
           ... update best
           if current_score < best_score - 1e-10: reset stalled; else stalled++
           if stalled > 200: break
           heat = max(heat_end, heat*0.997)
        if outer time < deadline: noise_amp*=1.5
```

Detailed `_fft_conv`:
```
def _fft_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n_out = 2*len(a)-1
    N = 1 << (n_out-1).bit_length()
    A = rfft(a, N)
    out = irfft(A*A, N)
    return out[:n_out]
```
Since A*A complex, irfft returns real. Use `A * A` not abs; for real convolution, yes square (not conjugate). `rfft` of a, square. Works for full convolution? rfft(a,N) gives DFT; square corresponds cyclic convolution; with zero pad enough length for linear. Good.

`_score_fast`:
```
def _score_fast(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf
    p=p/s
    b=_fft_conv(p)
    M=float(np.max(b))
    return 2.0*len(p)*M
```
This normalizes each time; in local p already sum1 but okay. Score invariant. Could avoid Normalization for speed in inner: `_score_fast_normalized(p)`. But use simple.

To reduce overhead in local, have `_b_and_score(p)` returns b,M,score assuming p sum1. We can normalize p after softmax. Use `score=2*n*M`.

`_softmax(z)`:
```
z = z - z.max()
e=np.exp(z)
s=e.sum()
if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full(n,1/n)
return e/s
```

`_choose_top_weights(b, K, heat, M)`:
```
L=len(b); K = min(K,L)
idx = np.argpartition(b, L-K)[L-K:] if K<L else np.arange(L)
top=b[idx]
if K==1: w=np.ones(1)
else:
   tau=max(1e-300, heat*M)
   logw=(top-M)/tau
   logw -= logw.max()
   w=np.exp(logw)
   w /= w.sum()
   # optionally zero very small
return idx, w
```

Local optimizer implementation:
```
def _local_optimize(p0, name, deadline, rng, K=64, heat_start=0.35, heat_end=0.02, step=0.12, beta=0.75, stall_limit=300, restart_budget=60.0):
    n=len(p0)
    best_p = p0.copy(); best_score = _score_fast_np1(p0)
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    noise_amp=0.02
    passes=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # initialize current from best with noise
        if noise_amp >0 and rng is not None:
           curr = best_p + noise_amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
           curr = np.maximum(curr, 1e-300)
           curr /= curr.sum()
        else: curr=best_p.copy()
        z=np.log(curr)
        momentum=np.zeros(n)
        heat=heat_start
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+restart_budget)
        stuck=0
        current_score=best_score
        it=0
        while time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:
            p=curr  # ensure sum1? maybe normalize
            p_sum=p.sum()
            if p_sum<=0: break
            p=p/p_sum
            b=_fft_conv(p)
            M=float(np.max(b))
            sc=2*n*M
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy(); stuck=0; noise_amp*=0.9? 
            else:
                stuck += 1
            if stuck > stall_limit: break
            if M<=0: break
            idx,w=_top_weights(b,K,heat,M)
            s=_shadow(p,idx,w)
            weighted_mean=float(np.dot(p,s))
            delta=s-weighted_mean
            scale=float(np.max(np.abs(delta))) if delta.size else 0.0
            if not finite or scale < 1e-300:
                # perturbation
                curr = ...; momentum=0; heat=max(heat_end, heat*0.8); continue
            d=delta/scale
            momentum = beta*momentum + (1-beta)*d
            z = z - step*momentum
            # occasionally recenter z
            z = z - z[0] if n>0 else z
            curr = _softmax(z)
            # normalize
            curr /= curr.sum()
            heat = max(heat_end, heat*0.996)
            it +=1
        # after pass, increase noise if not improved much? maybe
        noise_amp = min(0.2, noise_amp*1.3 + 0.005)
        restart_budget = min(120.0, restart_budget*1.1)
```

def _lowdim_refine(p0, deadline, heat=0.02, D=8):
    n=len(p0); x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    eps=1e-3
    basis=[]
    basis.append(x-0.5)
    basis.append((x-0.5)**2 - np.var(x))
    basis.append((x-0.5)**3)

    basis.append(-np.log(1-x+eps) - np.mean(-np.log(1-x+eps)))

    basis.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
    basis.append(np.sin(4*np.pi*x))
    # if D less, slice

    # ensure finite

```

```
theta=np.zeros(D)
best_theta=0; best_val=_lowdim_value(0,base_log,B,heat)
for iteration:
   grad=np.zeros(D)
   for j in range(D):
      h=0.05
      theta[j]+=h; valp=...
      theta[j]-=2h; valm=...
      theta[j]+=h
      grad[j]=(valp-valm)/(2h)
   # gradient step with adaptive step
   gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
   lr=0.1/gnorm if gnorm>0 else 0
   theta_new=theta-lr*grad
   theta_new=np.clip(theta_new,-2,2)
   val_new=_lowdim_value(theta_new,...)
   if val_new<best_val: theta=theta_new; best_theta=...
   else: lr*=0.5, etc
```

def _lowdim_value(theta, base_log, B, heat):
   u = base_log + B.dot(theta)
   u = np.clip(u, np.max(u)-50, np.max(u)) # stable
   p=np.exp(u); p/=p.sum()
   return _soft_score(p, heat)

def _random_smooth(n, rng, degree=6, amp=1.0):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    u=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(degree):
       freq = np.random.choice([1,2,3,4,5,6,8])? Need deterministic.
       a = rng.normal(0, amp/ (k+1))
       phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       u += a*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x + phase)
    p=np.exp(np.clip(u,-5,5))
    return p/p.sum()
```

`_shadow_from_weights`: Use `idx` and `w` arrays. idx int. Need sort? For shadow, if weights include zero small. Could sort by m for cache? Not necessary. But for negative slices, Python loop. Use local variables for speed.
```
def _shadow_from_weights(p, idx, w):
    n=len(p)
    s=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
       weight = float(w[ii])
       if weight <= 1e-15: continue
       m=int(idx[ii])
       lo=m-n+1
       if lo<0: lo=0
       hi=m
       if hi>=n: hi=n-1
       if hi<lo: continue
       start=m-lo
       stop=m-hi-1
       s[lo:hi+1] += weight * p[start:stop:-1]
    return s
```
Test for n=5, m=2, lo=0,hi=2,start=2,stop=-1, slice p[2:-1:-1] indices 2,1,0. s[0:3] gets p2,p1,p0 -> s_i = p_{2-i}. Good.
m=7,n=5, lo=3,hi=4,start=4,stop=2, p[4:2:-1] indices4,3. s[3:5] gets p[4] for i=3 (m-i=4); s[4]=p[3]. Good.

But if `stop` equals start? For one element, e.g., lo=hi=3,m=7,start=4,stop=2 -> slice indices4,3 length2! Wait lo=hi? If lo=hi=3, m-lo=4, m-hi-1=3? Actually m=7,hi=3 => stop=3. p[4:3:-1] index4 length1. My example hi=4 with lo=3 length2. Good. If lo=hi=3, hi< n? m=7,n=5, hi should min(n-1,m)=4, lo=m-n+1=3, so lo≠hi. okay.

However, if hi<m? Example m=1,n=5, lo=0,hi=1,start=1,stop=-1, p[1:-1:-1] should indices1,0? Let's be certain: `p[1:-1:-1]` with stop -1 (index 4?) def _lowdim_value(theta, base_log, B, heat):
    u = base_log + B.dot(theta)
    u -= u.max()
    # If theta large, exp ok
    p = np.exp(u)
    ps = p.sum()
    if ps <= 0: return np.inf
    p /= ps
    return _soft_score(p, heat)
```

def _soft_score(p, heat=0.05):
    n=len(p); b=_fft_conv(p); M=float(np.max(b))
    if M <= 0: return 0.0

def _score_normalized(p):
    n=len(p)
    if n==0: return np.inf
    b=_fft_conv(p)
    M=float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*M
```
Assumes p normalized; if sum not exactly 1, score should use sum. But we'll normalize. For raw candidates use `_score(a)` handles sum.
```
def _score_invariant(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15 return inf; a=a/s; return _score_normalized(a)
```

`_normalize` with target? Use `_normalize_to_one`.
```
def _to_prob(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: return np.full(a.size,1.0/a.size)
   return a/s
```

`_resample_distribution(p, n2)`:
```
def _resample_distribution(p, n2):
   n=len(p)
   p=_to_prob(p)
   if n==n2: return p
   # use cumulative inverse
   cdf=np.cumsum(p)
   cdf[-1]=1.0
   x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   # For quantile, use np.interp quarterly? For continuous distribution:
   q=np.linspace(0,1,n2)
   # to avoid endpoints, q=(arange(n2)+0.5)/n2
   q=(np.arange(n2)+0.5)/n2
   # interpolate cdf^{-1}: values indices /n
   # Use np.searchsorted then linear interp
   idx=np.searchsorted(cdf, q, side='right')
   idx=np.clip(idx,1,n)
   idx0=idx-1
   f=(q-cdf[idx0])/(cdf[idx]-cdf[idx0]+1e-300)
   x=(idx0 + f)/n
   # linear interpolate p over x
   y=np.interp(x, (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n, p)
   return y/y.sum()
```
If distribution is not smooth, fine.

def _init_uniform(n): return np.full(n,1.0/n)

def _init_exponential(n,beta):
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   u=beta*(x-0.5)
   u=u-u.max()
   p=np.exp(u); return p/p.sum()
```
With beta up to ±6; underflow okay.

`_init_power(n,a,left=True,eps=1e-4)`: x=(arange+0.5)/n; base=(x+eps) if left else (1-x+eps); p=base**(-a); normalize. For a negative? Use positive a.

`_init_smooth_random(n,rng, sing=0.0)`:
```
def _init_smooth_random(n, rng, degree=8, amp=1.0, sing_amp=1.0):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    u=0.5*(x-0.5)*rng.normal(0,amp) + ...
    eps=1e-3
    u += rng.normal(0,sing_amp)*(-np.log(x+eps) - np.mean(-np.log(x+eps)))
    u += rng.normal(0,sing_amp)*(-np.log(1-x+eps) - np.mean(-np.log(1-x+eps)))
    for k in range(2,degree+1):
       coeff = rng.normal(0, amp/(k+1))
       phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       u += coeff*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*(x-0.5) + phase)
    u -= u.mean()
    u = np.tanh(u) # to bound?
    p=np.exp(u)
    return p/p.sum()
```
Maybe exclude log to avoid extremes; sing_amp small 0.5.

Let's write `_local_optimize` with parameters. Need not update global best internally? Return.
```
def _local_optimize(p0, name, deadline, rng, K=64, heat_start=0.35, heat_end=0.02, step_init=0.08, step_decay=0.999, step_min=0.02,
                    beta=0.75, stall_limit=250, restart_budget=30.0, noise_amp_init=0.02, noise_amp_max=0.25):
   n=len(p0)
   best_p = _to_prob(p0)
   best_score = _score_normalized(best_p)
   if not np.isfinite(best_score): best_score=np.inf; best_p=_to_prob(np.ones(n))
   noise_amp = noise_amp_init
   pass_budget = min(restart_budget, max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if noise_amp > 0:
           # multiplicative noise
           lognoise = noise_amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
           lognoise -= lognoise.max()
           curr = best_p * np.exp(lognoise)
           curr = _to_prob(curr)
       else:
           curr=best_p.copy()
       z=np.log(curr)
       momentum=np.zeros(n)
       heat=heat_start; step=step_init
       sub_deadline=min(deadline, time.monotonic()+pass_budget)
       stuck=0
       it=0
       while time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:
           p=curr
           p_sum=p.sum()
           if p_sum<=0 or not finite: break
           p=p/p_sum
           b=_fft_conv(p)
           M=float(np.max(b))
           if not finite: break
           sc=2*n*M
           if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
               best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy(); stuck=0
           else:
               stuck += 1
           if stuck > stall_limit: break
           if M <=0: break
           idx,w = _top_weights(b, min(K, 2*n-1), heat, M)
           s=_shadow_from_weights(p, idx, w)
           mean=float(np.dot(p,s))
           delta=s-mean
           scale=float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
           if not finite or scale < 1e-300:
               # direct perturbation in p and reset
               curr = best_p * np.exp(0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)); curr=_to_prob(curr); z=np.log(curr); momentum=zeros; heat=max(heat_end,heat*0.9); step=step_init; stuck=0; continue
           d=delta/scale
           momentum=beta*momentum+(1-beta)*d
           z=z - step*momentum
           # keep z range
           z=z - z[0]
           curr=_softmax(z)
           curr_sum=curr.sum()
           if curr_sum<=0: break
           curr=curr/curr_sum
           heat=max(heat_end, heat*0.997)
           step=max(step_min, step*step_decay)
           it += 1
       noise_amp = min(noise_amp_max, noise_amp*1.25 + 0.01)
       pass_budget = min(90.0, pass_budget*1.1)
   return best_p,best_score
```
Use global `_softmax` and `_top_weights`. `rng` needs not be None; ensure.

Now, lowdim refine functions. Let's implement simple.
```
def _lowdim_p(theta, base_log, B):
   u=base_log + B.dot(theta)
   u-=u.max()
   p=np.exp(u)
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(len(u),1.0/len(u))
   return p/s

def _lowdim_value(theta, base_log, B, heat):
   p=_lowdim_p(theta, base_log, B)
   return _soft_score(p, heat)

def _lowdim_refine(p0, limit_s, heat=0.05, D=7):
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+limit_s
   n=len(p0); p0=_to_prob(p0)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   eps=1e-3
   basis=[]
   basis.append(x-0.5)
   basis.append((x-0.5)**2 - 1/12)
   basis.append((x-0.5)**3)
   lx=-np.log(x+eps); basis.append(lx - lx.mean())
   rx=-np.log(1-x+eps); basis.append(rx - rx.mean())
   basis.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*x))
   basis.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*x))
   basis.append(np.sin(4*np.pi*x))
   basis.append(np.cos(4*np.pi*x))
   arr=np.stack(basis)[:D]
   # normalize columns
   B=np.zeros((n,D))
   for j in range(D):
       col=arr[:,j]
       std=col.std()
       if std<1e-12: std=1.0
       B[:,j]=(col - col.mean())/std
   base_log=np.log(np.maximum(p0,1e-300))
   theta=np.zeros(D); best_theta=np.zeros(D)
   best_val=_lowdim_value(theta,base_log,B,heat)
   best_score=_score_normalized(p0); best_p=p0.copy()
   lr=0.4
   no_improve=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       h=0.1
       grad=np.zeros(D)
       for j in range(D):
           theta[j]+=h
           fp=_lowdim_value(theta,base_log,B,heat)
           theta[j]-=2*h
           fm=_lowdim_value(theta,base_log,B,heat)
           theta[j]+=h
           if finite fp,fm: grad[j]=(fp-fm)/(2*h)
       gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if not finite or gnorm<1e-10: break
       direction=grad/gnorm
       improved=False
       for frac in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125]:
           cand=theta - lr*frac*direction
           cand=np.clip(cand,-3,3)
           val=_lowdim_value(cand,base_log,B,heat)
           if finite val and val < best_val - 1e-8:
               theta=cand; best_val=val; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           lr *=0.5
           no_improve +=1
           if lr<1e-3 or no_improve>20: break
           continue
       no_improve=0
       p=_lowdim_p(theta,base_log,B)
       sc=_score_normalized(p)
       if finite sc and sc<best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_p=p; best_theta=theta.copy()
       # occasionally increase lr? not
   return best_p,best_score
```
Need `np.isfinite` for scalars. Good. This can be time if D=7 and each value FFT; 15 eval per iter. Limit 90s okay.

Potential issue: finite difference h=0.1 in θ; if objective scale ~1, fine. If gradient small, direction.

`_softmax` top-level:
```
def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size==0: return z
    z=z - np.max(z)
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       return np.full(z.shape, 1.0/z.size)
    return e/s
```
If z contains -inf, max finite, exp(-inf)=0. If all -inf (shouldn't), returns uniform.

def _weights_all(b, heat, M):
    tau=max(1e-300, heat*M)
    x=(b-M)/tau
    x-=x.max()

    s=w.sum()

    return w/s

```
def _init_smooth_random(n, rng, degree=8, amp=1.0, sing_amp=0.5):
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)
    u = rng.normal(0.0, amp * 0.6) * (x - 0.5)
    u += rng.normal(0.0, amp * 0.4) * ((x - 0.5) ** 2 - 1.0 / 12.0)
    u += rng.normal(0.0, amp * 0.3) * ((x - 0.5) ** 3)
    eps = 1e-3
    lx = -np.log(x + eps)
    rx = -np.log(1.0 - x + eps)
    u += rng.normal(0.0, sing_amp) * (lx - lx.mean())
    u += rng.normal(0.0, sing_amp) * (rx - rx.mean())
    for k in range(2, degree + 1):
       coeff = rng.normal(0.0, amp / float(k + 1))
       phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
       u += coeff * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * (x - 0.5) + phase)
    u = np.clip(u, -5.0, 5.0)
    p = np.exp(u)
    return _to_prob(p)
```

def _soft_score(p, heat=0.05):
    p=_to_prob(p)
    n=len(p)
    b=_fft_conv(p)
    M=float(np.max(b))
    if M <=0: return 0.0
    if heat <= 1e-8: return 2*n*M
    tau=heat*M
    x=(b-M)/tau
    x-=x.max()
    s=float(np.exp(x).sum())
    return 2*n*(M + tau*math.log(s))
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The solver works with probability vectors p (sum p = 1), because the objective
is scale invariant: score = 2n * max(convolve(p, p)).

Strategy:
  - Use FFT-based convolution for a fast internal score.
  - Start from the parent sequence if available, plus many smooth/asymmetric
    one-dimensional initializations (exponential, powers, random Fourier/log
    endpoint shapes).
  - Local optimization by mirror-descent on log-probabilities.
      * Top-k active convolution peaks are selected.
      * Weights on active peaks are chosen by a smooth max-approximation.
      * The gradient of the weighted packet of quadratic peak terms is formed.
      * Shannon-conditioned softmax gradient is used, with momentum, adaptive
        restarts, and noise.
  - A low-dimensional multiplicative refinement step is also used to polish
    the best sequence.
  - The best internal candidate is finally checked with the candidate-level
    evaluator, when available.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft


def _ffi_conv(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a using real FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n_out = 2 * len(a) - 1
    if n_out <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    nfft = 1 << (n_out - 1).bit_length()
    fa = rfft(a, nfft)
    out = irfft(fa * fa, nfft)
    return out[:n_out]


def _to_prob(a):
    """Coerce to a finite nonnegative probability vector."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _score_normalized(p):
    """Score assuming p is already a probability vector."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    b = _fft_conv(p)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * float(n) * m


def _score_invariant(a):
    """Score for arbitrary nonnegative vector, normalizing internally."""
    p = _to_prob(a)
    return _score_normalized(p)


def _softmax(z):
    """Stable softmax."""
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(z.shape, 1.0 / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _top_weights(b, K, heat, M):
    """Choose top-K peak indices and smooth weights over them."""
    L = len(b)
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(b, L - K)[L - K:]
        idx = idx.astype(np.int64, copy=False)
    top = b[idx]
    if K == 1:
        w = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        return idx, w
    tau = max(1e-300, float(heat) * float(M))
    x = (top - float(M)) / tau
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(top))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = w / s
    return idx, w


def _shadow_from_weights(p, idx, w):
    """
    s_i = sum_m w_m * p[m - i], valid indices only.
    This is the gradient (without factor 2) of sum_m w_m * b_m.
    """
    n = len(p)
    s = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for ii in range(len(idx)):
        weight = float(w[ii])
        if weight <= 1e-15:
            continue
        m = int(idx[ii])
        lo = m - n + 1
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = m
        if hi >= n:
            hi = n - 1
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        start = m - lo
        end = m - hi
        if end == 0:
            seg = p[start::-1]
        else:
            seg = p[start:end - 1:-1]
        s[lo:hi + 1] += weight * seg
    return s


def _weights_all(b, heat, M):
    """Smooth weights over all convolution peaks."""
    tau = max(1e-300, float(heat) * float(M))
    x = (b - float(M)) / tau
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(len(b), dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def _shadow_all(p, w):
    """
    Same as _shadow_from_weights but for a full weight vector w of length
    2n-1, computed by one extra FFT.
    """
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    required = 3 * n - 3
    if required <= 1:
        nfft = 1
    else:
        nfft = 1 << (required - 1).bit_length()
    wp = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    pr = rfft(wp, nfft)
    pq = rfft(p[::-1].copy(), nfft)
    c = irfft(pr * pq, nfft)
    start = n - 1
    end = 2 * n - 1
    if start >= len(c):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if end > len(c):
        end = len(c)
    out = c[start:end]
    if out.size < n:
        tmp = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        tmp[:out.size] = out
        return tmp
    return out


def _local_optimize(p0, name, deadline, rng,
                    K=64,
                    heat_start=0.35,
                    heat_end=0.02,
                    step_init=0.10,
                    step_decay=0.999,
                    step_min=0.02,
                    beta=0.75,
                    stall_limit=350,
                    restart_budget=40.0,
                    noise_amp_init=0.015,
                    noise_amp_max=0.30,
                    shadow_all=False):
    """
    Local Shannon mirror-descent optimizer with top-peak weighting, momentum,
    and periodic noisy restarts from the best point.
    """
    p0 = _to_prob(p0)
    n = len(p0)
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = _score_normalized(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = 2.0

    noise_amp = float(noise_amp_init)
    pass_budget = float(min(restart_budget, max(5.0, deadline - time.monotonic())))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if noise_amp > 1e-12 and rng is not None:
            ln = noise_amp * rng.standard_normal(n)
            ln -= float(np.max(ln))
            curr = best_p * np.exp(ln)
        else:
            curr = best_p.copy()

        curr = np.maximum(_to_prob(curr), 1e-300)
        curr = curr / float(curr.sum())
        z = np.log(curr)
        momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        heat = float(heat_start)
        step = float(step_init)
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + pass_budget)
        stuck = 0
        it = 0

        while time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:
            p = np.maximum(curr, 1e-300)
            ssum = float(p.sum())
            if ssum <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ssum):
                break
            p = p / ssum

            b = _fft_conv(p)
            M = float(np.max(b))
            if not np.isfinite(M):
                break

            sc = 2.0 * float(n) * M
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                stuck = 0
            else:
                stuck += 1

            if stuck > stall_limit:
                break
            if M <= 1e-300:
                break

            if shadow_all and heat > 0.15:
                w = _weights_all(b, heat, M)
                s_grad = _shadow_all(p, w)
            else:
                idx, w = _top_weights(b, K, heat, M)
                s_grad = _shadow_from_weights(p, idx, w)

            mean_p = float(np.dot(p, s_grad))
            delta = s_grad - mean_p
            scale = float(np.max(np.abs(delta)))
            if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-300:
                if rng is not None:
                    ln = 0.15 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                    ln -= float(np.max(ln))
                    curr = np.maximum(_to_prob(best_p * np.exp(ln)), 1e-300)
                    curr = curr / curr.sum()
                    z = np.log(curr)
                momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                heat = max(float(heat_end), heat * 0.9)
                step = float(step_init)
                stuck = 0
                continue

            d = delta / scale
            momentum = beta * momentum + (1.0 - beta) * d
            z = z - step * momentum
            z = z - float(z[0])

            curr = _softmax(z)
            curr = np.maximum(curr, 1e-300)
            csum = float(curr.sum())
            if csum <= 1e-300:
                break
            curr = curr / csum

            heat = max(float(heat_end), heat * 0.997)
            step = max(float(step_min), step * float(step_decay))
            it += 1

        noise_amp = min(float(noise_amp_max), noise_amp * 1.25 + 0.0075)
        pass_budget = min(90.0, pass_budget * 1.10)

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _init_uniform(n):
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def _init_exponential(n, beta):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    u = float(beta) * (x - 0.5)
    u = u - float(np.max(u))
    p = np.exp(u)
    return _to_prob(p)


def _init_power(n, alpha, left=True, eps=1e-4):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    base = x + eps if left else (1.0 - x) + eps
    p = base ** (-float(alpha))
    return _to_prob(p)


def _init_smooth_random(n, rng, degree=8, amp=1.0, sing_amp=0.55):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    u = rng.normal(0.0, 0.6 * amp) * (x - 0.5)
    u += rng.normal(0.0, 0.4 * amp) * ((x - 0.5) ** 2 - 1.0 / 12.0)
    u += rng.normal(0.0, 0.3 * amp) * ((x - 0.5) ** 3)

    eps = 1e-3
    lx = -np.log(x + eps)
    rx = -np.log(1.0 - x + eps)
    u += rng.normal(0.0, sing_amp) * (lx - float(np.mean(lx)))
    u += rng.normal(0.0, sing_amp) * (rx - float(np.mean(rx)))

    for k in range(2, degree + 1):
        coeff = rng.normal(0.0, amp / float(k + 1))
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        u += coeff * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * (x - 0.5) + phase)

    u = np.clip(u, -5.0, 5.0)
    p = np.exp(u)
    return _to_prob(p)


def _resample_distribution(p, n2):
    """Resample a discrete distribution to a new length by CDF interpolation."""
    p = _to_prob(p)
    n1 = len(p)
    if n1 == int(n2):
        return p
    cdf = np.cumsum(p)
    cdf[-1] = 1.0
    q = (np.arange(int(n2), dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n2)
    idx = np.searchsorted(cdf, q, side="right")
    idx = np.clip(idx, 1, n1).astype(np.int64)
    i0 = idx - 1
    denom = cdf[idx] - cdf[i0]
    denom = np.where(denom > 1e-300, denom, 1.0)
    frac = (q - cdf[i0]) / denom
    x_old = (np.arange(n1, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n1)
    x_new = (i0.astype(np.float64) + np.clip(frac, 0.0, 1.0)) / float(n1)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return _to_prob(y)


def _make_candidates(n, rng, max_cand=8):
    entries = []

    p = _init_uniform(n)
    entries.append((_score_normalized(p), "uniform", p))

    for beta in (-4.0, -2.5, -1.5, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.5, 4.0):
        p = _init_exponential(n, beta)
        sc = _score_normalized(p)
        entries.append((sc, "exp_%g" % beta, p))

    for alpha in (0.10, 0.25, 0.40, 0.48):
        for left in (True, False):
            p = _init_power(n, alpha, left=left, eps=1e-4)
            sc = _score_normalized(p)
            entries.append((sc, "pow%.2f_%d" % (alpha, 1 if left else 0), p))

    for r in range(4):
        p = _init_smooth_random(n, rng, degree=8, amp=1.0, sing_amp=0.55)
        sc = _score_normalized(p)
        entries.append((sc, "random_%d" % r, p))

    finite = []
    for e in entries:
        if e is not None and len(e) == 3 and np.isfinite(e[0]):
            finite.append(e)

    if not finite:
        p = _init_uniform(n)
        return [(_score_normalized(p), "uniform", p)]

    scores = []
    for e in finite:
        scores.append(float(e[0]))
    order = np.argsort(scores)

    out = []
    for oi in order:
        out.append(finite[int(oi)])
        if len(out) >= int(max_cand):
            break
    return out


def _update_best_score(new_score, new_p, best_score, best_p):
    if np.isfinite(new_score) and new_score < best_score:
        return float(new_score), _to_prob(new_p)
    return best_score, best_p


def _soft_score(p, heat=0.05):
    """Smoothed version of the score, useful for low-dimensional refinement."""
    p = _to_prob(p)
    n = len(p)
    b = _fft_conv(p)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0
    if heat <= 1e-8:
        return 2.0 * float(n) * M
    tau = float(heat) * M
    x = (b - M) / tau
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    s = float(np.exp(x).sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        s = 1.0
    return 2.0 * float(n) * (M + tau * math.log(s))


def _lowdim_p(theta, base_log, B):
    u = base_log + np.dot(B, theta)
    u = u - float(np.max(u))
    p = np.exp(u)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(u.shape, 1.0 / u.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s


def _lowdim_value(theta, base_log, B, heat):
    p = _lowdim_p(theta, base_log, B)
    return _soft_score(p, heat)


def _lowdim_refine(p0, time_limit_s, heat=0.05, D=7):
    """Low-dimensional multiplicative refinement around the current p."""
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(time_limit_s))
    p0 = _to_prob(p0)
    n = len(p0)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    eps = 1e-3

    basis = []
    basis.append(x - 0.5)
    basis.append((x - 0.5) ** 2 - 1.0 / 12.0)
    basis.append((x - 0.5) ** 3)
    lx = -np.log(x + eps)
    basis.append(lx - float(np.mean(lx)))
    rx = -np.log(1.0 - x + eps)
    basis.append(rx - float(np.mean(rx)))
    basis.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    basis.append(np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x))

    arr = np.stack(basis, axis=1)
    D = min(int(D), arr.shape[1])
    B = np.zeros((n, D), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(D):
        col = arr[:, j]
        std = float(np.std(col))
        if std < 1e-12:
            std = 1.0
        B[:, j] = (col - float(np.mean(col))) / std

    base_log = np.log(np.maximum(p0, 1e-300))

    theta = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_val = _lowdim_value(theta, base_log, B, heat)
    best_p = p0.copy()
    best_score = _score_normalized(best_p)

    lr = 0.45
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        h = 0.10
        grad = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
        ok = True
        for j in range(D):
            theta[j] += h
            fp = _lowdim_value(theta, base_log, B, heat)
            theta[j] -= 2.0 * h
            fm = _lowdim_value(theta, base_log, B, heat)
            theta[j] += h
            if not (np.isfinite(fp) and np.isfinite(fm)):
                ok = False
                break
            grad[j] = (fp - fm) / (2.0 * h)

        if not ok:
            break

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-10:
            break

        direction = grad / gnorm
        improved = False

        for frac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625):
            cand = theta - lr * frac * direction
            cand = np.clip(cand, -3.0, 3.0)
            val = _lowdim_value(cand, base_log, B, heat)
            if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val - 1e-8:
                theta = cand
                best_val = float(val)
                best_theta = theta.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            lr *= 0.5
            no_improve += 1
            if lr < 1e-3 or no_improve > 25:
                break
            continue

        no_improve = 0
        p = _lowdim_p(theta, base_log, B)
        sc = _score_normalized(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_p = p.copy()

    return best_p, float(best_score)


def _finalize_candidate(seq):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    seq = np.where(np.isfinite(seq), seq, 0.0)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(seq.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        seq = np.ones_like(seq)
    else:
        seq = seq / s
    return [float(v) for v in seq]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Generate search sequence under the given time budget.
    """
    max_budget = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(max_budget) or max_budget <= 0:
        max_budget = 2400.0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, max_budget - 12.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    had_parent = False
    parent = None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            parent = _to_prob(np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0))
            had_parent = True
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        had_parent = False

    if parent is None:
        parent = _init_uniform(8192)

    best_p = parent.copy()
    best_score = _score_normalized(best_p)
    print("initial best score: %.8f  n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_p)))

    # First: improve the parent / current best if it looks useful.
    if had_parent:
        budget0 = min(160.0, max(30.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.18))
        if budget0 > 15.0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
            sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + budget0)
            p2s, s2p = _local_optimize(
                best_p,
                "parent",
                sub_deadline,
                rng,
                K=64,
                heat_start=0.40,
                heat_end=0.04,
                step_init=0.10,
                step_decay=0.999,
                step_min=0.02,
                beta=0.75,
                stall_limit=350,
                restart_budget=40.0,
                noise_amp_init=0.015,
                noise_amp_max=0.30,
                shadow_all=False
            )
            best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(s2p, p2s, best_score, best_p)
            print("after parent local: %.8f  n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_p)))

    n_main = 8192

    # A uniform 8192 start is useful for symmetry breaking.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        p_u = _init_uniform(n_main)
        budgetu = min(80.0, max(20.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.10))
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + budgetu)
        p2u, s2u = _local_optimize(
            p_u,
            "uniform8192",
            sub_deadline,
            rng,
            K=96,
            heat_start=0.50,
            heat_end=0.05,
            step_init=0.12,
            step_decay=0.999,
            step_min=0.02,
            beta=0.80,
            stall_limit=350,
            restart_budget=40.0,
            noise_amp_init=0.03,
            noise_amp_max=0.35,
            shadow_all=False
        )
        best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(s2u, p2u, best_score, best_p)
        print("after uniform local: %.8f  n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_p)))

    # Candidate pool at 8192.
    entries = _make_candidates(n_main, rng, max_cand=10)
    if had_parent and len(parent) != n_main:
        pp = _resample_distribution(parent, n_main)
        sp = _score_normalized(pp)
        entries.append((sp, "parent_%d" % n_main, pp))
    elif had_parent and len(parent) == n_main:
        entries.append((_score_normalized(parent), "parent_exact", parent.copy()))

    if entries:
        scs = []
        for e in entries:
            scs.append(float(e[0]))
        order = np.argsort(scs)
        entries = [entries[int(i)] for i in order]

        # Keep only a few strong raw starts.
        n_trial = min(5, len(entries))
        for ti in range(n_trial):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 80.0:
                break
            sc_trial, name_trial, p_trial = entries[ti]
            if not np.isfinite(sc_trial):
                continue

            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            budget = min(90.0, max(25.0, remaining / max(1, n_trial - ti)))
            sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + budget)

            if ti % 2 == 0:
                cfg = dict(
                    K=64,
                    heat_start=0.35,
                    heat_end=0.03,
                    step_init=0.10,
                    step_decay=0.999,
                    step_min=0.02,
                    beta=0.75,
                    stall_limit=350,
                    restart_budget=35.0,
                    noise_amp_init=0.02,
                    noise_amp_max=0.30,
                    shadow_all=False
                )
            else:
                cfg = dict(
                    K=128,
                    heat_start=0.25,
                    heat_end=0.02,
                    step_init=0.13,
                    step_decay=0.999,
                    step_min=0.02,
                    beta=0.80,
                    stall_limit=350,
                    restart_budget=45.0,
                    noise_amp_init=0.025,
                    noise_amp_max=0.35,
                    shadow_all=False
                )

            p2c, s2c = _local_optimize(
                p_trial,
                name_trial,
                sub_deadline,
                rng,
                **cfg
            )
            before = best_score
            best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(s2c, p2c, best_score, best_p)
            print("candidate %s: %.8f -> best %.8f" %
                  (name_trial, s2c, best_score))

    # Extended refinement passes on the current best sequence.
    pass_configs = [
        dict(
            K=48,
            heat_start=0.35,
            heat_end=0.03,
            step_init=0.08,
            step_decay=0.999,
            step_min=0.02,
            beta=0.75,
            stall_limit=400,
            restart_budget=40.0,
            noise_amp_init=0.01,
            noise_amp_max=0.25,
            shadow_all=False
        ),
        dict(
            K=128,
            heat_start=0.25,
            heat_end=0.02,
            step_init=0.12,
            step_decay=0.999,
            step_min=0.02,
            beta=0.80,
            stall_limit=400,
            restart_budget=45.0,
            noise_amp_init=0.015,
            noise_amp_max=0.30,
            shadow_all=True
        ),
        dict(
            K=32,
            heat_start=0.15,
            heat_end=0.008,
            step_init=0.07,
            step_decay=0.999,
            step_min=0.015,
            beta=0.70,
            stall_limit=450,
            restart_budget=50.0,
            noise_amp_init=0.01,
            noise_amp_max=0.25,
            shadow_all=False
        ),
        dict(
            K=96,
            heat_start=0.50,
            heat_end=0.05,
            step_init=0.12,
            step_decay=0.999,
            step_min=0.02,
            beta=0.80,
            stall_limit=350,
            restart_budget=40.0,
            noise_amp_init=0.03,
            noise_amp_max=0.35,
            shadow_all=False
        ),
    ]

    for pi, cfg in enumerate(pass_configs):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 150.0:
            break
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
        budget = min(220.0, max(45.0, remaining * 0.22))
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + budget)
        p2pass, s2pass = _local_optimize(
            best_p.copy(),
            "best_pass_%d" % pi,
            sub_deadline,
            rng,
            **cfg
        )
        best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(s2pass, p2pass, best_score, best_p)
        print("best pass %d: %.8f  n=%d" % (pi, best_score, len(best_p)))

    # Low-dimensional multiplicative polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 80.0:
        ld_budget = min(130.0, max(35.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
        p2ld, s2ld = _lowdim_refine(best_p, ld_budget, heat=0.045, D=7)
        best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(s2ld, p2ld, best_score, best_p)
        print("after lowdim refinement: %.8f  n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_p)))

    # If the current best is not 8192, try a resampled 8192 version.
    if len(best_p) != n_main and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        p2r = _resample_distribution(best_p, n_main)
        sr = _score_normalized(p2r)
        best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(sr, p2r, best_score, best_p)
        budgetr = min(150.0, max(40.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0))
        sub_deadline_r = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + budgetr)
        p2r2, s2r2 = _local_optimize(
            p2r,
            "resample8192",
            sub_deadline_r,
            rng,
            K=96,
            heat_start=0.30,
            heat_end=0.025,
            step_init=0.11,
            step_decay=0.999,
            step_min=0.02,
            beta=0.78,
            stall_limit=400,
            restart_budget=45.0,
            noise_amp_init=0.02,
            noise_amp_max=0.30,
            shadow_all=False
        )
        best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(s2r2, p2r2, best_score, best_p)
        print("after resample8192: %.8f  n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_p)))

    # Final tiny polish if still enough time.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
        pnl = min(70.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
        p2f, s2f = _local_optimize(
            best_p.copy(),
            "final",
            min(deadline, time.monotonic() + pnl),
            rng,
            K=128,
            heat_start=0.18,
            heat_end=0.01,
            step_init=0.07,
            step_decay=0.9995,
            step_min=0.015,
            beta=0.80,
            stall_limit=500,
            restart_budget=40.0,
            noise_amp_init=0.008,
            noise_amp_max=0.20,
            shadow_all=False
        )
        best_score, best_p = _update_best_score(s2f, p2f, best_score, best_p)
        print("after final polish: %.8f  n=%d" % (best_score, len(best_p)))

    # Validate against the external evaluator if available and choose actual best.
    seq_best = _finalize_candidate(best_p)
    actual_best = seq_best
    actual_best_score = np.inf

    try:
        s_best = float(evaluate_sequence(seq_best))
        if np.isfinite(s_best):
            actual_best_score = s_best
            actual_best = seq_best
        print("external score of final best: %.8f" % s_best)
    except Exception as ey:
        print("external evaluation of final best failed: %s" % ey)

    if had_parent:
        seq_parent = _finalize_candidate(parent)
        try:
            s_parent = float(evaluate_sequence(seq_parent))
            if np.isfinite(s_parent) and s_parent < actual_best_score:
                actual_best_score = s_parent
                actual_best = seq_parent
            print("external score of parent: %.8f" % s_parent)
        except Exception as ey:
            print("external evaluation of parent failed: %s" % ey)

    print("returned internal best: %.8f, actual chosen: %.8f" %
          (best_score, actual_best_score if np.isfinite(actual_best_score) else float("inf")))

    return actual_best
```
